# Codebase Cleanup Inventory

Generated 2026-10-16 by the audit workflow (`temp/codebase-cleanup/audit-workflow.js`). Vocabulary and rules: `STYLE-GUIDE.md`.
This file lists findings only; nothing has been applied. Decide per row, then follow STYLE-GUIDE §5.

Legend for the `V` (verdict) column: **A** = verifier accepted the auditor's proposal as is. **M** = verifier modified it (the final text/name shown is the verifier's; the original proposal is in the notes). **B** = verifier reclassified it as a suspected bug: leave the comment and see §7.
No finding carries an `unverified` verdict: every naming and comment finding below went through the verify pass.

## 1. Summary

**Totals:** 87 naming findings (86 distinct renames: `_import_scope_expanded` was reported by two areas), 772 comment fixes, 79 glossary rows, 50 seed candidates, 13 suspected-bug entries (11 distinct). Areas not covered: []. Skeptic rejections (dropped by the verify pass, not listed here): {"naming":41,"comments":1}.

**Naming** by verdict: 68 A, 19 M. By scope: 8 cross-layer, 40 module, 39 local, 0 contract. By smell: misleading 44, stale 11, inconsistent 11, vague 9, missing-unit 4, abbreviation 3, wrong-shape 3, non-predicate-bool 2.

**Comments** by verdict: 660 A, 109 M, 3 B (left as is, see §7). By category: outdated 369, history-narration 200, dead-reference 137, wrong-docstring 51, stale-todo 13, commented-out-code 1, restates-code 1.

## 2. Glossary conflicts (feeds STYLE-GUIDE §3.3)

The `terms` lists from all ten areas (94 entries), merged into 78 concept rows where areas reported the same concept, plus row 11, added from a naming finding. "Suggested canonical" is a suggestion only. Names that are wire fields, DB columns, testids, Umami events or persisted values are contracts (STYLE-GUIDE §1): they stay even if another term wins, so the suggestion applies to internal identifiers and comments.

## 4. Contract-blocked names

No finding was classified with scope `contract`, so nothing is blocked. For awareness, the verifiers explicitly left these contract names untouched while renaming nearby identifiers:

- `StoreBotGameRequest.bot_elo` wire field (the `normalization.py` parameter rename in §5 does not touch it).
- `metrics_elo` wire SectionId (only the internal `_METRICS_ELO_BUCKETS` constant is renamed).
- `shield_level` API field and `train-shield-*` testids (UI says "freezes").
- testids kept as is: `variation-tree-desktop`, `mini-board`, `train-landing-host`, `bots-intro`, `chip-tactic-*-{id}` (value pattern), `drawer-filter-sidebar`, `btn-open-bookmark-sidebar`, `drawer-bookmark-sidebar`, `btn-suggest-bookmarks-sidebar`, `btn-bookmark-sidebar`, `btn-signup-why-*`, the `data-window` attribute.
- `TrainSessionResult` / `GradeResult` field names in the sessionStorage reveal cache (flagged by fe-hooks as possible contracts; no rename proposed).
- DB column names inside `FLAW_BLOB_COLUMNS` (the tuple is renamed; its values are not).
- `EnqueueTier1Response.status` value 'skipped_guest' (a wire Literal that is no longer produced; listed as seed #1 in §7).

## 6. Comment fixes, by area then category

Columns: location | V | current excerpt (shortened) | replacement (verifier's final text; "delete" means remove the comment). Frontend paths are relative to `frontend/src/`. Categories follow STYLE-GUIDE §2.2: wrong-docstring, outdated, dead-reference, stale-todo, history-narration, commented-out-code, restates-code. Bare planning tags were not reported (the §2.1 mechanical pass handles them).

| `app/services/guest_cleanup_service.py:118` | M | Phase 224 D-08 supersedes the Phase 189 D-04 preservation FOR GUESTS ONLY: Phase 224 removed the Train guest gate (`_reject_guest`) | Guests can accumulate drill_sessions/drill_solves/train_settings rows, and /welcome advertises the 30-day inactivity purge as a sign-up difference, which is only honest if a purged guest's Train state goes with their games. Deleting drill_sessions is enough: drill_solves.session_id is ON DELETE CASCADE, which reaches warm-up filler and red-herring solves whose game_id is NULL or a stranger's game (rows the games cascade cannot see). Do NOT add a separate DrillSolve delete. The is_guest re-check above guarantees this never reaches a registered user, whose Train rows are preserved. |
| `app/services/train_reminder_service.py:3` | A | Plan 201-01 built the notification-copy layer ... Plan 201-04 (this addition) adds the periodic tick | Train reminder scheduler: notification copy (`build_reminder_payload`, also used by the dev-only trigger endpoint `POST /api/push/dev/trigger-reminder`) plus the periodic tick: `run_periodic_train_reminders` is an asyncio task in the FastAPI lifespan, shaped like guest_cleanup_service (a named interval constant, a per-tick orchestrator `send_due_reminders` that opens its own session per candidate, and a single aggregate Sentry capture per tick). Per-candidate step order (`_process_candidate`) is load-bearing: |
| `app/services/train_reminder_service.py:151` | A | Deviation from the plan's literal "reuse is_scheduled_day without special-casing" instruction -- see 201-04-SUMMARY.md's Deviations section | A weekday_mask of 0 ('train anytime') must never fire a reminder, but is_scheduled_day(day, 0) is True (every day counts as scheduled for pool eligibility), so mask 0 is special-cased here. |
| `app/services/push_send.py:17` | M | SEED-135 D1 (2026-08-03): the prune branch itself is now ALSO logged ... Phase 204 D5 (2026-08-03): `_PUSH_TTL_SECONDS = 0` was wrong. | Only a 404/410 response means 'prune this subscription'. The prune branch logs a warning and sends an info-level capture_message (never capture_exception: 404/410 is the normal end of a subscription's life; do not restore an error-level capture). Its context carries push_host (host only, never the path, which is a bearer capability), user_agent, user_id and the subscription id; the message is a fixed literal. Every other non-2xx status and every transport error is logged and capture_exception-ed, with no retry and no deletion. ttl_seconds is computed by the caller (train_scheduler.seconds_until_end_of_local_day); this module does no timezone math. Do not use TTL 0: a discarded TTL-0 message returns 201 exactly like a delivery. |
| `app/services/email_service.py:120` | A | Opens its own `email_http_client()` inside the spawned coroutine — the caller no longer opens one. | Opens its own `email_http_client()` inside the spawned coroutine. |

Read at call time (a module global, never a default argument) so tests can monkeypatch it. Starts 2026-09-28 so the launch week (board live Sunday 2026-10-04) awards medals; finalization recomputes from drill_solves, so a past week finalizes exactly. |

Batch size is not memory-bound: positions use binary COPY and the eval pass runs in the cold drain (don't move it back into this lane: a per-batch Stockfish pass here drove a Postgres OOM at batch 28). The import bottleneck is the eval drain, which batch size does not affect. |

A completed scheduled-day session ticks the shield/count up (capped at SHIELD_CAP); a missed scheduled day drains the shield by one (floored at 0, resetting the count when it empties); a scheduled day before the user had trainable material is neutral. apply_result's fail_count is a LIFETIME lapse counter with two park doors: Door A (never solved + 3 lifetime lapses) and Door B (6 lifetime lapses regardless of ever_correct, see LEECH_FAIL_THRESHOLD). Door B exists because an item that alternates solved/failed never reaches MASTERED (3 consecutive correct) and is past Door A after its first correct solve.' |

## 7. Seed candidates and suspected bugs

Verbatim from the auditors and verifiers. No action taken; the owner decides what becomes a seed (no seeds were created).

### 7.1 Seed candidates (50)

**backend-surface**
1. `app/schemas/imports.py:79` — EnqueueTier1Response.status still lists 'skipped_guest', but no path produces it: enqueue_tier1_game lets guests enqueue (QUEUE-08 gate opened). The Literal is repeated in routers/admin.py:113 and frontend/src/types/api.ts:205. Removing it is a cross-layer contract change, not a one-liner.
2. `app/routers/eval_remote.py:1` — Comment debt from the Phase 149-03 Gen-1 /lease+/submit deletion is spread across routers/eval_remote.py (about 14 present-tense references to _apply_submit or /submit) and schemas/eval_remote.py (SubmitEval/SubmitResponse/'old pair stays deprecated'). It needs one coordinated rewrite of the atomic-lane docstrings, not piecemeal edits.
3. `app/schemas/train.py:1` — The train schema docstrings (module, TrainPuzzle, SolveRequest, SolvedResult, PuzzleRevealResponse, SolveResponse) are layered by phase (189 → 206 → 211 → 235 → 236), and later layers contradict earlier ones about what the client knows pre-attempt. Rewriting them as one current contract is larger than a comment fix per line.

**backend-repositories**
4. `app/repositories/llm_log_repository.py:144` — get_latest_log_by_hash has no production caller (only tests reference it), because get_latest_successful_log_for_user replaced it on the hot path. Removing it, its docstring references and its tests in tests/test_llm_log_repository.py is more than a one-line fix.
5. `app/repositories/library_repository.py:1989` — fetch_flaw_comparison hand-writes 24 near-identical player/opp COUNT FILTER columns, while _tempo_phase_count_columns already generates the crossed ones from a table. Generating the marginals the same way would remove ~160 lines (refactor, not a comment fix).

**services-eval-engine**
6. `app/services/eval_apply.py:2144` — _recently_flaw_completed_users is write-only: _signal_flaw_completion adds to it on every completed game (from eval_drain and eval_remote), but nothing reads it, and the 'Phase 118 cache invalidation' it waits for was never wired. It is an unbounded per-process set. Either wire the intended invalidation or delete the set and the hook (3 call sites).
7. `app/services/eval_apply.py:187` — _FullPlyEvalTarget.best_move is a dead field: it is never set by either constructor and never read (best_move travels in engine_result_map/dedup_map). Remove it together with its misleading docstring.
8. `app/services/eval_queue_service.py:594` — Tier-3 derived claim is a plain SELECT with no lock, and the TODO says to add FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED 'when multi-worker leasing is added'. Remote workers now lease tier-3 via scope='idle', so double-claims waste engine work. This needs a design (a TTL lease on games, or SKIP LOCKED inside the ES lottery).
9. `app/services/eval_drain.py:66` — eval_drain re-exports ~10 eval_apply/eval_entry symbols with noqa F401 'backward-compat' markers, and only tests (plus scripts/remote_eval_worker.py for _walk_pv_boards) use them. Migrate those imports to the defining modules and drop the re-export blocks.
10. `app/services/eval_apply.py:216` — Underscore-prefixed names form the real cross-module API of the eval pipeline (_FullPlyEvalTarget, _collect_full_ply_targets, _build_flaw_blob_lease_positions, _claim_tier4_blob/_claim_tier4_bestmove, _collect_midgame_eval_targets, ...). They are imported by eval_drain, eval_remote, import_service and scripts. Promoting them to public names is a multi-file rename (mock.patch targets in tests too).
11. `app/services/eval_apply.py:1499` — _classify_with_overlay's overlay=False branch has no caller and is kept 'for a future direct-classify caller'. Removing the parameter (all 3 callers pass overlay=True) would shrink a documented trap.
12. `app/services/eval_apply.py:2719` — _apply_bestmove_submit keeps a transient fallback for old workers that omit best_cp/best_mate (re-searches candidate plies server-side, 'stops firing once every worker submits best_cp'). Add a removal trigger: check worker_heartbeats schema/sf versions, then delete the stale_targets branch.
13. `app/services/eval_drain.py:117` — _RETRIABLE_DB_OUTAGE_ERRORS is a verbatim copy of import_service._RETRIABLE_DB_OUTAGE_ERRORS (comment: 'Copied verbatim'). Move it into a shared module (e.g. app/core/database.py) so the two drains can't drift.

**services-insights-stats**
14. `app/services/insights_llm.py:1764` — `cohort_anchors` is threaded through _render_summary_block / _render_subsection_block / _render_layout_item but never used ('Kept in the signature for backwards compat'). Remove the parameter and its plumbing.
15. `app/services/opening_insights_service.py:387` — The `se` element is threaded through _row_to_finding → _dedupe_within_section → _dedupe_continuations → _rank_section but explicitly ignored ('preserved for upstream compatibility'). Drop the tuple element and simplify the section types.
16. `app/services/insights_service.py:99` — _TIMELINE_SUBSECTION_IDS is defined but never read in insights_service (insights_llm.py:367 owns its own copy, and a test redefines a third). Delete it or make insights_llm import the one copy.
17. `app/services/endgame_zones.py:938` — assign_per_class_zone has no caller in app/, tests/ or scripts/, and NOTABLE_ENDGAME_ELO_DIVERGENCE_THRESHOLD (line 181) is unreferenced. Prune the dead zone helpers and constants.
18. `app/services/canonical_slice_sql.py:235` — elo_bucket_expr and sparse_exclusion_sql have no production or script caller (the cited _build_per_bucket_sanity_query is gone); only tests import them. ### 7.2 Suspected bugs (13 entries, 11 distinct)

1. **backend-surface** `app/schemas/normalization.py:49` — Platform-reported accuracy/ACPL is written into the canonical computed columns. NormalizedGame.white_accuracy/black_accuracy/white_acpl/black_acpl hold the chess.com/lichess-reported values (normalization.py:308, :481), and import_service._flush_batch inserts g.model_dump() unchanged via bulk_insert_games, so they land in games.white_accuracy/white_acpl. models/game.py:187-205 documents those columns as 'our uniform lichess-formula computed ... NULL until computed' and the *_imported columns as 'platform-reported values preserved at import'. `git grep accuracy_imported -- app` finds no writer for *_imported (only the migration copied old values). Result: unanalyzed games carry platform values in the canonical columns that library_service serves as 'our' accuracy (schemas/library.py:141-146), and new imports never fill *_imported.
2. **services-eval-engine** `app/services/eval_queue_service.py:1091` — Tier-4 blob claims return is_lichess_eval_game=False with the comment 'resolved in Plan-03 lease handler', but the bundled scope=None path's stated sole consumer is the in-process drain (comment at lines 1049-1051). _full_drain_tick special-cases only TIER_BESTMOVE_BACKFILL and otherwise uses claimed.is_lichess_eval_game as-is (eval_drain.py:1367, 1407, 1566, 1594). Tier-4's predicate (full_evals_completed_at IS NOT NULL AND blobs_completed_at IS NULL) matches lichess-eval games, which tier-3 branch (b) stamps. For such a game the drain would therefore build a terminal donor, use the opening dedup cache, write engine post-move evals over the lichess %evals, and donate them to the opening cache.
3. **services-insights-stats** `app/services/opening_insights_service.py:641` — `samples = [(int(r.sample_pair[1]), int(r.sample_pair[0])) for r in raw_rows]` indexes sample_pair before any None check. query_opening_transitions documents sample_pair as NULL when every game in the group is custom-start (openings_repository.py:599-604, 663-666), 'which the service layer drops', and _wrap_transition_row (line 500) has that guard. But this list comprehension runs first and would raise TypeError, failing the whole compute_insights call (re-raised at line 1042).
4. **fe-components-train-analysis-bots** `frontend/src/components/bots/ResumeGate.tsx:110` — The identity line renders `${plyCount} moves`, but the prop doc (L37-39) says plyCount is the hook's liveGamePly, a half-move count. A game with 10 full moves shows '20 moves'. Either divide by 2 or label it plies.
5. **fe-components-data-views** `frontend/src/components/library/MoveStats.tsx:378` — The gameId doc (:146-153) says every data-testid gets the -${gameId} suffix so several MoveStats instances on one page get distinct testids. The mobile expand toggle hard-codes data-testid="move-stats-expand-toggle" without tid(), so every Library game card on a mobile list renders the same testid.
6. **fe-components-rest-and-root** `frontend/src/components/filters/TacticDepthFilter.tsx:82` — `isSummaryActive = activePreset !== 'medium'` with comment 'not the always-on Medium default', but the default is High (DEPTH_DEFAULT_PRESET = 'high', lib/tacticDepth.ts:78; DEFAULT_TACTIC_DEPTH_VALUE = High). The summary is therefore highlighted at the default and un-highlighted when the user narrows to Medium. Likely should compare against DEPTH_DEFAULT_PRESET.
7. **fe-components-rest-and-root** `frontend/src/components/move-explorer/MoveExplorer.tsx:49` — IS_TOUCH uses `'ontouchstart' in window`; ChessBoard.tsx:475-480 documents that Chrome sets this on touch-capable desktops (touchscreen laptops). On such desktops a mouse click on a move row only highlights it and a second click is needed to play, contrary to the 'Desktop: single click plays the move' branch. Plausible, not verified on hardware.
8. **fe-pages-api-types-lib** `frontend/src/api/client.ts:111` — isAuthRoute checks whether error.config.url starts with '/api/auth/'. apiClient's baseURL is '/api' and requests use relative URLs like '/auth/jwt/login' and '/auth/google/authorize', so config.url never carries the '/api' prefix and isAuthRoute is always false. A 401 from an auth endpoint sent with a token (e.g. a guest refresh or promote) can then trigger the session-expired redirect. Fix: test startsWith('/auth/').
9. **fe-pages-api-types-lib** `frontend/src/types/activity.ts:1` — ActivityStatsPayload claims to mirror the backend Payload but has no guest_train field. Backend app/services/activity_queries.py sends it and pages/activity/render.js reads payload.guest_train, so the type has drifted from the payload.
10. **fe-pages-api-types-lib** `frontend/src/pages/Import.tsx:242` — The done-state copy renders `${n} games imported.`, which the comment at L233-236 forbids (Phase 96: must not say 'Imported N games' because evals are still running). Either the copy regressed or the rule is obsolete. *Resolved by the comment verifier: not a bug. Commit 8577cf806 deliberately reintroduced the count; the comment is the stale side (see §6 `pages/Import.tsx:233`).*
11. **fe-pages-api-types-lib** `frontend/src/types/activity.ts:1` — Confirmed: the comment is correct and the type is incomplete. Python Payload (app/services/activity_queries.py:120) has `guest_train: dict[str, Any]`, render.js:44 reads `payload.guest_train`, but ActivityStatsPayload has every other Payload key and lacks guest_train. Adding the field is a type change outside comment cleanup, so flag it for the owner. *(Duplicate of #9, from the comment verifier.)*
12. **fe-pages-api-types-lib** `frontend/src/pages/openings/useOpeningsHandlers.ts:64` — The comment is right that default chart entries are gone (chartBookmarks = bookmarks.filter(...), useOpeningsChartData:117), but that makes the `else if (mostPlayedData)` branch it labels (useOpeningsHandlers.ts:63-69) likely dead code, not just a stale comment. Deleting only the comment would leave an unexplained dead branch. Flag it for the owner to remove the branch (out of scope for comment cleanup).
13. **fe-pages-api-types-lib** `frontend/src/lib/engine/ortRuntimeSource.ts:74` — The comments match their constants (13_961_845 / 25_749_873 at 1.29.0), but the constants themselves are stale. The vendored files are 13,479,978 / 24,254,953 bytes, and frontend/package.json and public/maia/README pin onnxruntime-web 1.27.0. Fixing the comment alone would make it contradict the values; updating the values is a code change outside comment cleanup. The impact is minor (pre-Content-Length progress placeholder).
